<script>
/* 本轮降价复核独立展示；不回写旧审批、销量、利润或后台价格。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  if (root.markdownFollowupModel?.ready) return;
  const source = document.getElementById('markdownReviewData');
  const pane = document.getElementById('planPane');
  if (!source || !pane) throw new Error('降价复核数据或页面未载入');
  const data = JSON.parse(source.textContent || '{}');
  if (!Array.isArray(data.rows) || !data.rows.length) throw new Error('降价复核清单为空');
  const rows = data.rows;
  const bySku = new Map(rows.map(row => [row.sku, row]));
  if (bySku.size !== rows.length || rows.some(row => !row.sku)) throw new Error('降价复核SKU重复或缺失');
  const meta = data.meta || {};
  const asOf = meta.asOf || '';
  const colors = { red: '红色 · 11/17已降', purple: '紫色 · 120天策略', green: '绿色 · 已降', gray: '灰色 · 规则观察', unmarked: '未标色', orange: '原橙色 · 刚到仓' };
  const actionLabels = {
    second_round: '第二轮复核', wayfair: '转Wayfair复核', observe_period: '观察期内',
    executed_observe: '已降，继续观察', gray_monitor: '按灰色规则观察', quote_review: '先核报价',
    sales_review: '先核销量', scope_review: '先核执行范围', normal_review: '常规复核', arrival_review: '刚到仓观察',
  };
  const pendingActions = new Set(['second_round', 'wayfair', 'quote_review', 'scope_review', 'sales_review']);
  const queueDefinitions = [
    { id: 'pending', label: '待处理', matches: row => pendingActions.has(row.action) },
    { id: 'all', label: '全部', matches: () => true },
    { id: 'second', label: '第二轮', matches: row => row.action === 'second_round' },
    { id: 'wayfair', label: 'Wayfair', matches: row => row.action === 'wayfair' },
    { id: 'observe', label: '观察中', matches: row => ['observe_period', 'executed_observe'].includes(row.action) },
    { id: 'gray', label: '灰色监控', matches: row => row.color === 'gray' },
    { id: 'over120', label: '超120天非紫色', matches: row => Boolean(row.over120) && row.color !== 'purple' },
    { id: 'focus', label: '未标色超120天', matches: row => row.focusUnmarked === true },
  ];
  const state = { queue: 'pending', color: '', search: '' };
  const escape = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[char]);
  const known = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const number = value => known(value) ? value.toLocaleString('zh-CN', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) : '待核';
  const money = value => known(value) ? '$' + value.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) : '待核';
  const date = value => value ? String(value).slice(0, 10) : '待核';
  const quoteDate = value => value ? date(value) : '未取得有效报价';
  const shortDate = value => value && date(value).startsWith(String(asOf).slice(0, 4) + '-') ? date(value).slice(5) : date(value);
  const fileName = value => String(value || '').split(/[\\/]/).pop();
  const label = row => row.actionLabel || actionLabels[row.action] || '待复核';
  const plainRule = rule => typeof rule === 'string' ? rule : rule?.label || rule?.description || rule?.definition || rule?.rule || '';
  function interval(lower, upper) {
    if (known(lower) && known(upper)) return lower === upper ? number(lower) : number(lower) + '—' + number(upper);
    if (known(lower)) return '≥' + number(lower) + '（上界待核）';
    if (known(upper)) return '≤' + number(upper) + '（下界待核）';
    return '待核';
  }
  function colorLabel(row) { return colors[row.color] || '未标色'; }
  function conditionalLabel(row) { return row.conditionalDates ? '条件日期：' : ''; }
  function baseFiltered(filters) {
    const query = String(filters.search || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return rows.filter(row => (!filters.color || row.color === filters.color) && (!query || [row.sku, row.stage, label(row), row.reason, row.scopeLabel, row.price?.platform, row.price?.store, ...((row.history || []).map(event => event.store))].some(value => String(value || '').toLowerCase().includes(query))));
  }
  function filtered(override) {
    const filters = { ...state, ...(override || {}) };
    const queue = queueDefinitions.find(item => item.id === filters.queue) || queueDefinitions[0];
    return baseFiltered(filters).filter(queue.matches);
  }
  function postActionText(row, verbose = false) {
    const post = row.postAction || {};
    if (post.covered === false && (post.note || post.requestedFrom)) return { title: '待覆盖', body: verbose ? post.note || ('请求从' + date(post.requestedFrom || post.from) + '开始；销量仅覆盖至' + date(post.through || meta.salesThrough) + '，不按0销量处理。') : ((post.requestedFrom || post.from) ? shortDate(post.requestedFrom || post.from) + '起观察 · ' : '') + '销量至' + shortDate(post.through || meta.salesThrough) };
    if (!post.from) return { title: '观察起点待核', body: verbose ? post.note || '按本SKU实际执行日期核定观察窗口。' : '需核实际执行起点' };
    if (post.covered !== true) return { title: '待覆盖', body: verbose ? post.note || ('请求从' + date(post.from) + '开始；销量仅覆盖至' + date(post.through || meta.salesThrough) + '，不按0销量处理。') : shortDate(post.from) + '起观察 · 销量至' + shortDate(post.through || meta.salesThrough) };
    const maturity = post.windowComplete === false ? '；按通知日计算尚未覆盖15天' : post.windowComplete === true ? '；按通知日计算已覆盖15天' : '';
    return { title: interval(post.lower, post.upper) + '套', body: (verbose ? date(post.from) : shortDate(post.from)) + '—' + (verbose ? date(post.through) : shortDate(post.through)) + (known(post.observedDays) ? ' · 已覆盖' + number(post.observedDays) + '天' : '') + maturity + (post.scopeLabel ? ' · ' + post.scopeLabel : '') + (verbose && post.note ? '；' + post.note : '') };
  }
  function allChannelsText(row, verbose = false) {
    const all = row.postAction?.allChannels;
    if (!all) return '';
    if (all.covered === false || !known(all.lower) && !known(all.upper)) return '全渠道参考：' + (verbose ? all.note || '待覆盖' : '待覆盖');
    return '全渠道参考：' + interval(all.lower, all.upper) + '套' + (all.from && all.through ? '（' + (verbose ? date(all.from) : shortDate(all.from)) + '—' + (verbose ? date(all.through) : shortDate(all.through)) + '）' : '') + (verbose && all.scopeLabel ? ' · ' + all.scopeLabel : '') + (verbose && all.note ? '；' + all.note : '');
  }
  function observationText(row) {
    const window = row.observationStart && row.observationEnd ? date(row.observationStart) + '—' + date(row.observationEnd) : '观察起止待核';
    return conditionalLabel(row) + window + (row.nextReviewDate ? '；复核' + date(row.nextReviewDate) : '；下次日期待核');
  }
  function batchText(row) {
    return row.mixedOldBatch ? '本轮仅旧批' + number(row.actionQuantity) + '套；另' + number(row.unaffectedNewSets) + '套新批不在本轮清仓范围' : '';
  }

  const panel = document.createElement('section');
  panel.id = 'markdownReviewPanel';
  panel.className = 'mdr-panel';
  panel.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'mdrTitle');
  panel.innerHTML = '<div class="mdr-heading"><div><h2 id="mdrTitle">降价与清仓复核</h2><p class="mdr-dates" id="mdrDates"></p></div><div class="mdr-tools"><button type="button" id="mdrExport">导出当前筛选</button><button type="button" id="mdrCopy">复制运营文案</button></div></div>'
    + '<p class="mdr-rule">红色按用户确认已于11月17日降价，绿色保留已降状态。紫色按首降20%、观察15天、再减原价10%（累计30%）、仍卖不出转Wayfair逐阶段复核。</p>'
    + '<div class="mdr-tabs" id="mdrTabs" aria-label="降价复核队列"></div>'
    + '<div class="mdr-filters"><label>颜色<select id="mdrColor" aria-label="按颜色筛选"><option value="">全部颜色</option>' + Object.entries(colors).map(([key, value]) => '<option value="' + escape(key) + '">' + escape(value) + '</option>').join('') + '</select></label><label class="mdr-search-label">搜索<input id="mdrSearch" type="search" placeholder="SKU、店铺或处理结论" aria-label="搜索降价复核"></label><button type="button" id="mdrReset">重置筛选</button><span id="mdrCount" class="mdr-count" aria-live="polite"></span></div>'
    + '<div class="mdr-table-scroll"><table class="mdr-table"><colgroup><col style="width:175px"><col style="width:330px"><col style="width:220px"><col style="width:145px"><col style="width:180px"><col style="width:250px"><col style="width:230px"><col style="width:225px"></colgroup><thead><tr><th>SKU／本轮标记</th><th>处理结论／规则</th><th>现货／库龄</th><th>近30／60天净套</th><th>执行／历史通知</th><th>调后销量证据</th><th>观察／下次复核</th><th>报价／已降参考</th></tr></thead><tbody id="mdrRows"></tbody></table><div id="mdrEmpty" class="mdr-empty" hidden>当前筛选没有SKU。</div></div>'
    + '<p class="mdr-message" id="mdrMessage" role="status"></p><div id="mdrCopyFallback" class="mdr-copy-fallback" hidden><label for="mdrCopyText">可选中文案后复制</label><textarea id="mdrCopyText" rows="12" readonly></textarea></div>';
  pane.insertBefore(panel, pane.firstChild);

  const historical = document.createElement('details');
  historical.className = 'mdr-history';
  historical.id = 'mdrHistoricalSnapshot';
  historical.innerHTML = '<summary>原审批与分析快照 · 业务日11月12日</summary><div class="mdr-history-body"><p class="mdr-history-note">以下保留原审批与分析记录：业务日11/12，原销量至11/8。当前处理结论以本页上方复核清单为准。</p></div>';
  const historyBody = historical.querySelector('.mdr-history-body');
  const priorPanel = document.getElementById('leaderPanel');
  const priorAnalysis = document.getElementById('leaderAnalysis');
  if (priorPanel || priorAnalysis) {
    if (priorPanel) historyBody.appendChild(priorPanel);
    if (priorAnalysis) historyBody.appendChild(priorAnalysis);
    panel.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', historical);
  }
  const dialog = document.createElement('dialog');
  dialog.id = 'mdrDialog';
  dialog.className = 'mdr-dialog';
  dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'mdrDetailTitle');
  dialog.innerHTML = '<div class="mdr-dialog-head"><h2 id="mdrDetailTitle"></h2><button type="button" data-mdr-close aria-label="关闭SKU复核详情">关闭 ×</button></div><div id="mdrDetailBody" class="mdr-dialog-body"></div>';
  document.body.appendChild(dialog);
  const el = id => document.getElementById(id);
  let detailOpener = null;
  function setMessage(value) { el('mdrMessage').textContent = value; }
  function badge(row) { return '<span class="mdr-badge mdr-color-' + escape(row.color || 'unmarked') + '">' + escape(colorLabel(row)) + '</span>'; }
  function rowHtml(row) {
    const post = postActionText(row), price = row.price || {};
    const references = Array.isArray(row.executedReferenceQuotes) ? row.executedReferenceQuotes : [];
    const executedReference = references.find(item => item.primary) || references[0] || {};
    const shownPrice = row.color === 'red' ? executedReference : price;
    const priceCaption = row.color === 'red' ? '已降参考（按11/16报价算，待逐店回读）' : [price.platform, price.store].filter(Boolean).join(' / ');
    const executionCaption = row.executedDate ? '已执行 ' + date(row.executedDate) : row.color === 'green' ? '历史已降，日期待核' : '观察／历史记录';
    return '<tr data-mdr-row="' + escape(row.sku) + '"><td><button type="button" class="mdr-sku" data-mdr-sku="' + escape(row.sku) + '">' + escape(row.sku) + '</button>' + badge(row) + (row.focusUnmarked ? '<span class="mdr-focus">超120天重点</span>' : '') + '</td>'
      + '<td><strong>' + escape(label(row)) + '</strong><small>' + escape(row.stage || '') + '</small><p class="mdr-reason" title="' + escape(row.reason || '') + '">' + escape(row.reason || '') + '</p>' + (row.color === 'gray' && plainRule(row.grayRule) ? '<small class="mdr-gray-rule">规则：' + escape(plainRule(row.grayRule)) + '</small>' : '') + '</td>'
      + '<td><strong>' + number(row.available) + '套</strong><small>' + escape(row.ageText || '库龄待核') + '</small>' + (batchText(row) ? '<small class="mdr-batch">' + escape(batchText(row)) + '</small>' : '') + (row.scopeLabel ? '<small>' + escape(row.scopeLabel) + '</small>' : '') + '</td>'
      + '<td><span>30天：' + escape(interval(row.net30Lower, row.net30Upper)) + '</span><small>60天：' + escape(interval(row.net60Lower, row.net60Upper)) + '</small><small>最近有效单：' + escape(date(row.lastValidOrderDate)) + '</small></td>'
      + '<td><strong>' + escape(executionCaption) + '</strong><small>' + escape(row.executionBasis || '未以通知日代替实际执行日') + '</small><small>通知：' + escape(date(row.noticeDate)) + '</small></td>'
      + '<td><strong class="' + ((row.postAction || {}).covered === true ? '' : 'mdr-pending') + '">' + escape(post.title) + '</strong><small>' + escape(post.body) + '</small>' + (allChannelsText(row) ? '<small>' + escape(allChannelsText(row)) + '</small>' : '') + '</td>'
      + '<td><span>' + escape(observationText(row)) + '</span>' + (row.conditionalDates ? '<small class="mdr-pending">实际执行时间确认后，观察节点随之确定。</small>' : '') + '</td>'
      + '<td><strong>' + money(shownPrice.total) + '</strong><small>' + escape(priceCaption) + '</small>' + (row.color === 'red' ? '<small>' + escape(executedReference.store || '店铺待核') + '</small>' : '') + '<small>商品 ' + money(shownPrice.item) + ' ＋ 运费 ' + money(shownPrice.shipping) + '</small><small>' + (row.color === 'red' ? '参考报价日：' : '报价日期：') + escape(quoteDate(row.color === 'red' ? executedReference.referenceDate : price.date)) + '</small>' + (references.length ? '<small>' + references.length + '条已降参考，点SKU查看</small>' : '') + '</td></tr>';
  }
  function render() {
    const base = baseFiltered(state), visible = filtered();
    el('mdrDates').textContent = '复核' + date(asOf) + ' · 库存' + date(meta.inventoryDate) + ' · 销量至' + date(meta.salesThrough) + ' · 历史报价' + date(meta.quoteDate);
    el('mdrTabs').innerHTML = queueDefinitions.map(queue => '<button type="button" data-mdr-queue="' + queue.id + '" class="' + (state.queue === queue.id ? 'active' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (state.queue === queue.id) + '">' + queue.label + '<span>' + base.filter(queue.matches).length + '</span></button>').join('');
    el('mdrRows').innerHTML = visible.map(rowHtml).join('');
    el('mdrEmpty').hidden = visible.length > 0;
    const knownInventory = visible.filter(row => known(row.available));
    el('mdrCount').textContent = '显示' + visible.length + '款 / 本轮' + rows.length + '款；已知可用' + number(knownInventory.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.available, 0)) + '套' + (knownInventory.length < visible.length ? '，另' + (visible.length - knownInventory.length) + '款数量待核' : '');
    el('mdrColor').value = state.color;
    if (el('mdrSearch').value !== state.search) el('mdrSearch').value = state.search;
    return visible;
  }
  function table(headers, tableRows) {
    return '<div class="mdr-detail-table-scroll"><table class="mdr-detail-table"><thead><tr>' + headers.map(value => '<th>' + escape(value) + '</th>').join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' + tableRows.map(values => '<tr>' + values.map(value => '<td>' + escape(value) + '</td>').join('') + '</tr>').join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
  }
  function openDetail(sku, opener) {
    const row = bySku.get(sku);
    if (!row) return false;
    detailOpener = opener || document.activeElement;
    el('mdrDetailTitle').textContent = row.sku + ' · ' + label(row);
    const post = postActionText(row, true), price = row.price || {}, history = Array.isArray(row.history) ? row.history : [];
    let html = '<div class="mdr-detail-summary">' + badge(row) + '<h3>' + escape(label(row)) + '</h3><p>' + escape(row.reason || '') + '</p>' + (row.scopeLabel ? '<p>执行／判断范围：' + escape(row.scopeLabel) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    html += '<h3>阶段、日期与销量</h3>' + table(['项目', '本次记录'], [
      ['阶段', row.stage || '待核'], ['已执行日期', row.executedDate ? date(row.executedDate) : '未取得'],
      ['执行日期依据', row.executionBasis || '待核；不以通知日代替'], ['历史通知日期', date(row.noticeDate)],
      ['观察窗口', observationText(row)], ['日期条件', row.conditionalDates ? '依实际执行日确认的条件日期' : '按本次已记录日期'],
      ['销量截止', date(meta.salesThrough)], ['执行范围调后销量', post.title + '；' + post.body],
      ['全渠道参考', allChannelsText(row, true) || '未提供，执行范围和全渠道不混算'],
      ['近30天净量', interval(row.net30Lower, row.net30Upper) + '套'], ['近60天净量', interval(row.net60Lower, row.net60Upper) + '套'],
      ['最近有效订单日', date(row.lastValidOrderDate)], ['当前可用库存', number(row.available) + '套'], ['库龄', row.ageText || '待核'],
      ...(row.mixedOldBatch ? [['本轮旧批范围', batchText(row)]] : []),
    ]);
    if (row.grayRule) html += '<h3>灰色监控规则</h3><p>' + escape(plainRule(row.grayRule) || '按本SKU规则与已覆盖销量持续复核。') + '</p>';
    html += '<h3>报价参考</h3>' + table(['平台／店铺', '商品价', '运费', '含运价', '报价日', 'Listing'], [[
      [price.platform, price.store].filter(Boolean).join(' / ') || '待核', money(price.item), money(price.shipping), money(price.total), quoteDate(price.date), price.listing || '待核',
    ]]);
    const references = Array.isArray(row.executedReferenceQuotes) ? row.executedReferenceQuotes : [];
    if (references.length) html += '<h3>已降价格参考</h3><p class="mdr-detail-note">按本次用户确认记为已降。下列参考用于保留执行信息，不能再次作为5%待执行方案。</p>' + table(['店铺', '商品价参考', '原运费', '含运价参考', '参考日', '说明'], references.map(item => [item.store || '待核', money(item.item), money(item.shipping), money(item.total), date(item.referenceDate), item.note || '']));
    html += '<h3>调价历史</h3>' + (history.length ? table(['记录日期', '类型', '平台／店铺', '记录降幅', '调整前', '调整后', '状态与说明', '来源'], history.map(item => [
      date(item.date), item.kind || '', [item.platform, item.store].filter(Boolean).join(' / '), known(item.rate) ? (item.rate * 100).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + '%' : '',
      known(item.before) ? money(item.before) : '', known(item.after) ? money(item.after) : '', [item.status, item.text].filter(Boolean).join('；'), item.source || '',
    ])) : '<p>本次已留存来源未检出明确调价记录，不能据此认定从未降价。</p>');
    html += '<p class="mdr-detail-source">分类来源：' + escape(fileName(row.sourceFile) || '本轮库存复核表') + (row.sourceCell ? ' · ' + escape(row.sourceCell) : '') + '</p>';
    el('mdrDetailBody').innerHTML = html;
    if (typeof dialog.showModal === 'function') { if (!dialog.open) dialog.showModal(); }
    else dialog.setAttribute('open', '');
    return true;
  }
  function closeDetail() {
    if (typeof dialog.close === 'function') dialog.close();
    else dialog.removeAttribute('open');
    if (detailOpener && typeof detailOpener.focus === 'function') detailOpener.focus();
  }
  const csvHeaders = ['库存SKU', '本轮颜色', '处理结论', '阶段', '判断原因', '全国可用套', '库龄', '30日净下界', '30日净上界', '60日净下界', '60日净上界', '最近有效订单日', '历史通知日', '已执行日', '执行依据', '观察开始', '观察结束', '下次复核日', '是否条件日期', '判断范围', '调后窗口开始', '调后覆盖至', '调后片段已覆盖', '调后净量下界', '调后净量上界', '调后窗口说明', '平台', '店铺', 'Listing', '报价商品价USD', '报价运费USD', '报价含运价USD', '报价日', '重点未标色超120天', '灰色规则', '来源文件', '来源单元格', '复核日期', '销量截止', '按通知日计算15天已覆盖', '已覆盖观测天数', '全渠道参考', '本轮处理数量', '不受影响新批套数', '混龄仅旧批'];
  function exportRows() {
    return filtered().map(row => { const post = row.postAction || {}, price = row.price || {}; return [
      row.sku, colorLabel(row), label(row), row.stage, row.reason, row.available, row.ageText, row.net30Lower, row.net30Upper, row.net60Lower, row.net60Upper,
      row.lastValidOrderDate, row.noticeDate, row.executedDate, row.executionBasis, row.observationStart, row.observationEnd, row.nextReviewDate,
      row.conditionalDates ? '是' : '否', row.scopeLabel, post.from, post.through, post.covered === true ? '是' : '否', post.covered === true ? post.lower : null,
      post.covered === true ? post.upper : null, post.note || (post.covered === true ? '' : '待覆盖，不按0处理'), price.platform, price.store, price.listing,
      price.item, price.shipping, price.total, price.date || null, row.focusUnmarked ? '是' : '', plainRule(row.grayRule), fileName(row.sourceFile), row.sourceCell, asOf, meta.salesThrough,
      post.windowComplete === true ? '是' : post.windowComplete === false ? '否' : '待核', post.observedDays, allChannelsText(row, true),
      row.actionQuantity, row.unaffectedNewSets, row.mixedOldBatch ? '是' : '',
    ]; });
  }
  function csvValue(value) {
    let text = value == null ? '' : String(value);
    if (typeof value !== 'number' && /^[=+\-@]/.test(text)) text = "'" + text;
    return '"' + text.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
  }
  function csvText() { return '\ufeff' + [csvHeaders, ...exportRows()].map(values => values.map(csvValue).join(',')).join('\r\n'); }
  function exportCsv() {
    const content = csvText(), name = '降价复核_' + String(asOf || '本轮').replace(/-/g, '') + '_' + state.queue + '.csv';
    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' }), url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const anchor = document.createElement('a'); anchor.href = url; anchor.download = name; document.body.appendChild(anchor); anchor.click(); anchor.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    setMessage('已导出当前筛选' + filtered().length + '款SKU。');
    return { name, count: filtered().length, text: content };
  }
  function operationsText() {
    const visible = filtered();
    const lines = ['@所有人 各位运营同事，以下为' + date(asOf) + '降价复核结论（当前筛选' + visible.length + '款）：'];
    const actionOrder = ['second_round', 'wayfair', 'quote_review', 'scope_review', 'sales_review', 'observe_period', 'executed_observe', 'gray_monitor', 'arrival_review', 'normal_review'];
    const actions = [...new Set([...actionOrder, ...visible.map(row => row.action)])];
    for (const action of actions) {
      const group = visible.filter(row => row.action === action);
      if (!group.length) continue;
      lines.push('', (actionLabels[action] || group[0].actionLabel || '复核') + '（' + group.length + '款）');
      for (const row of group) lines.push(row.sku + '：' + label(row) + '。' + (row.reason || '') + (row.nextReviewDate ? ' ' + (row.conditionalDates ? '条件复核日' : '复核日') + date(row.nextReviewDate) + '。' : ''));
    }
    lines.push('', '红色已于11月17日降价，绿色为已降，均避免重复同一轮折扣。清仓策略：先20%，观察15天，再减原价10%至累计30%，后续仍卖不出再转Wayfair。', '销量证据截至' + date(meta.salesThrough) + '；未覆盖的调后日期待补，不按0销量判断。');
    return lines.join('\n');
  }
  async function copyOperations() {
    const content = operationsText();
    try {
      if (!navigator.clipboard?.writeText) throw new Error('Clipboard unavailable');
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(content);
      el('mdrCopyFallback').hidden = true;
      setMessage('已复制当前筛选' + filtered().length + '款的运营文案。');
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      const area = el('mdrCopyText'); area.value = content; el('mdrCopyFallback').hidden = false; area.focus(); area.select();
      try { if (document.execCommand('copy')) { setMessage('已复制当前筛选运营文案。'); return true; } } catch (_) {}
      setMessage('文案已展开，可选中后复制。'); return false;
    }
  }
  function setFilters(value) {
    if (value.queue != null && queueDefinitions.some(queue => queue.id === value.queue)) state.queue = value.queue;
    if (value.color != null) state.color = colors[value.color] ? value.color : '';
    if (value.search != null) state.search = String(value.search);
    return render();
  }
  panel.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const queue = event.target.closest('[data-mdr-queue]');
    if (queue && panel.contains(queue)) { state.queue = queue.dataset.mdrQueue; render(); }
    const sku = event.target.closest('[data-mdr-sku]');
    if (sku && panel.contains(sku)) openDetail(sku.dataset.mdrSku, sku);
  });
  el('mdrColor').addEventListener('change', event => { state.color = event.target.value; render(); });
  el('mdrSearch').addEventListener('input', event => { state.search = event.target.value; render(); });
  el('mdrReset').addEventListener('click', () => { Object.assign(state, { queue: 'pending', color: '', search: '' }); render(); setMessage('已恢复本轮待处理清单。'); });
  el('mdrExport').addEventListener('click', exportCsv);
  el('mdrCopy').addEventListener('click', copyOperations);
  dialog.querySelector('[data-mdr-close]').addEventListener('click', closeDetail);
  dialog.addEventListener('click', event => { if (event.target === dialog) closeDetail(); });
  const goSources = document.getElementById('goSources');
  if (goSources) goSources.addEventListener('click', () => { if (!pane.hidden && historical.parentElement) historical.open = true; });
  const oldCsv = typeof leaderCsv === 'function' ? leaderCsv : null;
  if (oldCsv) leaderCsv = function () {
    if (typeof pageState !== 'undefined' && pageState.view === 'plans') return exportCsv();
    return oldCsv.apply(this, arguments);
  };
  function planSubtitle() {
    return '库存' + date(meta.inventoryDate) + ' · 销量至' + date(meta.salesThrough) + ' · 报价' + date(meta.quoteDate) + ' · 复核' + date(asOf);
  }
  function refreshPlanHeader() {
    if (typeof pageState === 'undefined' || pageState.view !== 'plans' || pane.hidden) return;
    const title = document.querySelector('h1'), subtitle = document.getElementById('subtitle');
    if (title && title.textContent !== '清仓与促销') title.textContent = '清仓与促销';
    const expected = planSubtitle();
    if (subtitle && subtitle.textContent !== expected) subtitle.textContent = expected;
  }
  const beforePageView = typeof setPageView === 'function' ? setPageView : null;
  if (beforePageView) setPageView = function () {
    const result = beforePageView.apply(this, arguments);
    refreshPlanHeader();
    return result;
  };
  const navigation = document.querySelector('.page-nav');
  if (navigation) navigation.addEventListener('click', () => queueMicrotask(refreshPlanHeader));
  const subtitle = document.getElementById('subtitle');
  if (subtitle && typeof MutationObserver === 'function') {
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (typeof pageState !== 'undefined' && pageState.view === 'plans' && !pane.hidden && subtitle.textContent !== planSubtitle()) queueMicrotask(refreshPlanHeader);
    });
    observer.observe(subtitle, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true });
  }
  root.markdownFollowupModel = {
    ready: true, version: meta.version || asOf, meta, rules: data.rules || {}, rows, bySku, state,
    queues: queueDefinitions.map(({ id, label }) => ({ id, label })), filtered, baseFiltered: () => baseFiltered(state),
    render, setFilters, openDetail, closeDetail, exportCsv, exportRows, csvText, csvHeaders, operationsText, copyOperations, refreshPlanHeader,
  };
  render();
  refreshPlanHeader();
  const marker = document.createElement('span'); marker.id = 'markdownReviewReady'; marker.hidden = true; document.body.appendChild(marker);
})(window);

</script>
